# EODHD backfill priority seed. Written 2026-08-15T23:22:14 by Claude Code.
#
# READ THIS BEFORE TRUSTING THE ORDER BELOW.
#
# This file sets tier 0 of the queue build, which controls the order symbols are
# ENQUEUED. It does NOT control the order they are FETCHED. The drain pulls its
# work straight from the manifest with "ORDER BY interval, ticker, window_start"
# (research_lab/fetcher/manifest.py, pending_units), which is plain alphabetical.
# run_backfill's own docstring claims "every phase walks the most useful names
# first"; that is false as written and is a real defect in the fetcher, found
# 2026-08-15. Until it is fixed, a seed file cannot prioritise anything.
#
# So priority is currently enforced in the MANIFEST instead: on 2026-08-15 the
# 7,299 pending 5-minute and 1-hour units belonging to currently-LISTED tickers
# were set to status 'deferred', which the drain's predicate does not match, so
# only DELISTED work remains in the queue. Tamer's call: delisted history is the
# part that can disappear from a vendor, the live names will still be there when
# the subscription is renewed. enqueue() is ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING, so the parked
# units survive every future run until they are deliberately restored.
#
# TO RESTORE THE PARKED UNITS (one statement, no code):
#   UPDATE fetch_unit SET status='pending' WHERE status='deferred';
# Backup taken before the change: /mnt/ssd/eodhd/manifest.db.bak-2026-08-15-preDeferLive
#
# The list below is the definition of the intended universe, not a fetch order.
